##### What was that? Instagram CEO gets real about AI Slop, while the Microsoft chief does the opposite. Meanwhile, Grok AI floods X with sexualized photos, and the rest is just marketing at its...

You know what, let’s start over. It’s for the best. 2026, baby!?

* [Instagram CEO](https://substack.com/redirect/c45a28e3-306e-4dc5-9452-bfd20c62038b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g): AI is so ubiquitous ‘it will be [more practical](https://substack.com/redirect/b6a302b9-4219-4ce1-9ffd-3596ef0cd152?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to fingerprint real media than fake media’
* Snapchat launches new prompt-based “[Animate It](https://substack.com/redirect/09d410a1-35a2-4d13-b8b1-49b139e3b94b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” lens for paid subscribers.

	+ No, Grok can’t really “[apologize](https://substack.com/redirect/882bac48-cb72-4b55-b519-76c33fd7cd20?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” for posting non-consensual sexual images
* Pinterest stock [rises](https://substack.com/redirect/e0a0da19-0ac8-4a85-8119-ae933a5f1a9f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) after [The Information](https://substack.com/redirect/344c9efe-a80d-4ec8-881a-8ae3aa3667b3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) predicts OpenAI acquisition.

> *In partnership with [Tracksuit](https://substack.com/redirect/9e758b3a-a8de-4498-b2a1-3cd37998247c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)*

##### **The Bimma x Tracksuit: [2025 Collab of the Year Report](https://substack.com/redirect/6ce90d4b-33fb-4029-aeb3-e8768ba7492a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is finally here…**

For the first time, [Bimma Williams](https://substack.com/redirect/2fc18039-4f89-4a43-a7b7-4b799559b306?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) partnered with Tracksuit to measure sneaker collaborations the way consumers actually experience them, with real data and real cultural insight. They [analyzed](https://substack.com/redirect/6ce90d4b-33fb-4029-aeb3-e8768ba7492a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) the drops that moved people, shifted conversations, and built real brand energy.

And built the [COLLAB Index](https://substack.com/redirect/6ce90d4b-33fb-4029-aeb3-e8768ba7492a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g): an evaluation tool that assesses collaborations across six dimensions: Chemistry, Originality, Legacy, Leadership, Audience Engagement, and Brand Energy.

Most brands still pick collaborators based on vibes, but vibes alone don’t sell anything. The [COLLAB Index](https://substack.com/redirect/6ce90d4b-33fb-4029-aeb3-e8768ba7492a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) blends two data sources into one clear score that shows what worked *and* why:

* Consumer Reality: Tracksuit data from a survey of 2,000+ qualified US participants.
* Cultural Reality: Evaluation from Bimma’s network of creators, journalists and strategists

> *It’s the simplest way for marketers to understand, and prove, what makes a collaboration truly effective. If you’re planning a brand collab in 2026, don’t pitch it without this [Collab of the Year](https://substack.com/redirect/6ce90d4b-33fb-4029-aeb3-e8768ba7492a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) report.*

* [Vera Bradley](https://substack.com/redirect/e0f6e211-7972-4c07-8b2c-54f1c8cc37fe?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) goes back to its whimsical roots.
* [Advertising](https://substack.com/redirect/5f8b4794-2a5b-4950-9a00-72077a621291?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) will boom in 2026, but Hollywood is at risk of being left behind.
* [China’s BYD](https://substack.com/redirect/4bd6807c-51dc-42db-998e-b91d8dce8038?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) overtakes Tesla as world’s top EV seller.
* How [protesters](https://substack.com/redirect/0674848c-b4db-49b2-802b-453ab1851c8f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) became content for the cops.
* NYC phone ban reveals some students [can’t read](https://substack.com/redirect/1ee97363-1f49-4e7d-b76d-9b5e55d45c85?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) clocks.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!sjUU!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fa21cfcaf-a721-407c-a9db-778b1aff1e95_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/2f0a26db-a10f-4eb9-a596-9be0d2f0da4d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* TikTok Shop launches [digital gift cards](https://substack.com/redirect/178b4b19-57fe-42fe-a9f7-371b3fa70dbe?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to challenge Amazon and eBay.
* TikTok introduces [TikTok GamePlan](https://substack.com/redirect/ace8e04b-e966-4f9e-bf10-d471b79d4a3e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), a comprehensive product suite for sports media and brands.
* [Poland](https://substack.com/redirect/18433ee0-9fe7-4ca7-968c-3ab3252a132d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) calls for EU action against [AI-generated TikTok videos](https://substack.com/redirect/9dea0222-dead-4811-97f4-60d531e2008a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) calling for “Polexit”.
	+ European Union [responds](https://substack.com/redirect/8392f90d-1b34-43ae-aede-86d7297453f1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to TikTok videos calling for [Polexit](https://substack.com/redirect/5aa73dd7-6c54-493e-9478-435730fd9c8c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* TikTok returns as the official entertainment partner of the [International Ski Federation (FIS)](https://substack.com/redirect/87a2fe9f-78c4-451d-9e17-70242768444a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) after its premiere last year.
* TikTok’s Chinese owner plans [$23bn AI spend](https://substack.com/redirect/5d1db8b0-0789-408d-b6d7-9730183f1416?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to keep pace with US rivals.
* TikTok’s new US owners [won’t control](https://substack.com/redirect/889c42b0-e238-4772-9c30-dc70218c418d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) key parts of the business, according to a leaked memo.
* TikTok [added](https://substack.com/redirect/482d074c-3780-454a-8920-fc7548db0ef0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) seasonal animations, personalized Year in Review summaries.
* TikTok Shop Malaysia to [intensify](https://substack.com/redirect/fc9b6d48-0ca8-4f61-b3d2-6f18c1216421?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) over [1,000 All-Star Live Sessions](https://substack.com/redirect/85d4cdb0-d51f-46c3-953e-7d6bb78f2fd1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in 2026.
* ‘[A gamechanger](https://substack.com/redirect/91aefd84-4343-4623-98e1-1274a18f3700?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’: 200,000 UK small businesses sign up to TikTok Shop.
* China’s TikTok rival flooded with [porn and violent video](https://substack.com/redirect/59fb4be3-20af-42c7-924e-c04339f84baf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in cyberattack.
* TikTok is jumping into [micro dramas](https://substack.com/redirect/c2754c79-8f7e-4fe4-9a44-b36f242629cc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), the surging bite-sized soaps that have caught Hollywood’s eye.
* Did Starmer [impose a curfew](https://substack.com/redirect/544b5481-2f05-4c2c-8306-a2d7297ea681?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in the U.K.? No, It’s a fake TikTok video.
* TikTok wants more creators to take a chance on brand campaigns. So it’s [offering](https://substack.com/redirect/a3b5d7e8-140a-4360-99f5-5db1162ba9f2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) $10,000.
* After watching X’s ownership issues play out, marketers brace for [TikTok whiplash](https://substack.com/redirect/f8110798-d68d-470f-a005-99bedd06a037?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in 2026.
* ByteDance’s [StoryMem](https://substack.com/redirect/30301b3c-e2b1-44a0-a794-19b1115a3dd8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) gives AI video models a memory so characters stop shapeshifting between scenes.
* ICYMI: Tiktok is scheduled to [change ownership](https://substack.com/redirect/8c1ea08c-1f60-4137-a5fc-d815bec3f7b6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on January 22, 2026.

* What a [14-year-old Instagram sensation](https://substack.com/redirect/e6bd5516-2c00-4f3a-9e38-6c16843ba001?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) and six-hitting superstar tells us about life and cricket.
* Instagram Stories is testing a new Close Friends feature for the [Top 5 close friends](https://substack.com/redirect/52ac7d08-8305-4986-8db4-c1b445b6ac71?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* How [Meta’s Reels](https://substack.com/redirect/b4d43dcb-ac9a-47df-a45c-d832c3777712?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) became a $50 billion business.
	+ Instagram’s EU growth [eclipses](https://substack.com/redirect/d97678fa-48b2-488b-9fb0-899b1dc3a1cd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Facebook.
* Adam Mosseri calls out [camera companies](https://substack.com/redirect/3c184513-2693-4b13-bfd7-b9df135b5844?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for going in the wrong direction.
* [North West](https://substack.com/redirect/0408689b-5080-4f5b-8bab-ad5b3c0d611e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is on Instagram.
* Instagram on iOS is testing [a new tab for profiles](https://substack.com/redirect/af6f57f4-4641-4ee2-97e8-328f394db06b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), and it could be for playlists or possibly a reposts feed, similar to what TikTok tested a while ago.

> 🦀 Report Break: Deloitte predicts that global ad revenue for “[podcasts and vodcasts](https://substack.com/redirect/4eccccfd-c26c-41f8-9c83-1f415ca892e5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” will reach $5bn in 2026, up 20% year-on-year. 

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!VXYk!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F54241e92-dc00-45e3-b0a1-422988e5b58f_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/c2b1b211-e485-4a8f-bf4f-834456f25b41?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* [Italy Watchdog](https://substack.com/redirect/3c0139af-6dee-4201-9cc1-7bd3fba86489?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) orders Meta to allow [rival AI Chatbots](https://substack.com/redirect/3d2fe809-4e97-49a7-a1be-a887b206ea0c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in WhatsApp.
* Meta says it will fully [sever Manus’s China](https://substack.com/redirect/c6e96625-150f-45a6-80b8-59293dd21437?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) ties after acquiring the AI startup.
* [Meta Ads Library](https://substack.com/redirect/5d69c0ff-cb34-42fd-b87e-08e394284d9c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is now showing a label 'Low impression count' for ads that have generated less than 100 impressions.
* WhatsApp calls out [restrictions in Russia](https://substack.com/redirect/24fc8ecd-c350-4a27-a023-3dad4f94ee58?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) after reported slowdown.
* Meta’s Pixio proves [simple pixel reconstruction](https://substack.com/redirect/5addafa1-e61b-438c-b946-ac69dfb146b4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) can beat complex vision models.
* WhatsApp may soon allow [channel admins](https://substack.com/redirect/ad4fb264-d0d0-4c72-9f69-0da3a4577e77?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to share invite links with contacts on iOS.
* This [ad-free Facebook app](https://substack.com/redirect/c1458d40-b5f7-4a6c-b18a-76aac84e2960?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) made my feed enjoyable again for the first time in years.
* 8 WhatsApp features [to](https://substack.com/redirect/fb70df89-940e-4ec5-bcb7-9c59e94de048?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) [boost](https://substack.com/redirect/fb70df89-940e-4ec5-bcb7-9c59e94de048?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) your security and privacy.
* Apple, Meta ship significantly [fewer VR headsets](https://substack.com/redirect/13c06e48-f1e6-4b1f-b70f-d9e752a40f2e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in 2025.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!xdrk!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F504f7135-86f7-4edd-a923-b08f71d3eeb3_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/b64c6f06-642c-4817-816e-5d69564f13af?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* More than [20% of videos](https://substack.com/redirect/531935c1-125d-43f2-b569-72aeca891422?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) shown to new YouTube users are ‘[AI slop](https://substack.com/redirect/74b25f7b-52fe-4e02-890b-052860be0a4d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’, study finds.
* Do [faces](https://substack.com/redirect/01f760e1-7ba2-4878-8a2e-ed6c63af25e8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) help YouTube thumbnails? Here’s what the data says.
	+ In a recent post, [vidIQ suggested](https://substack.com/redirect/aaf70dc2-6d62-4f6b-be4f-7cc44a28d140?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) that unless you’re already well-known, people click for ideas rather than creators, and that removing your face from thumbnails can raise CTR.
* The [relentless](https://substack.com/redirect/abd14265-6993-492b-904d-74a4684a165c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) rise of YouTube.
* Seriously, Google? [Uncensored porn](https://substack.com/redirect/a8c35311-02f5-4158-9b8b-e4b7ce7fca09?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) still appears on YouTube.
* This 22-year-old college dropout makes [$700,000 a year](https://substack.com/redirect/a0cf55ac-71b5-47e9-8cbe-330154147460?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) from ‘AI slop’ people sleep through.
* YouTube [launch](https://substack.com/redirect/9fd98ce2-9019-4409-8513-29a460ff0399?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) recap 2025 campaign​.
* YouTube TV is [getting $20 cheaper](https://substack.com/redirect/d9c1be27-36c9-41ca-9769-d490d96ad698?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for select users thanks to a new partnership.

> 🫒 Report Break: The Italian TV market is projected to reach a value of [€8.97 billion by the end of 2025](https://substack.com/redirect/99c3e93a-72ec-4287-9395-b071ca85401f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), representing a 2.2% growth over 2024.

> While linear TV advertising is projected to experience a decline of 0.4% in 2025, non-linear online advertising is set to register nearly 30% growth.

* Google lowers [audience size limits](https://substack.com/redirect/c8031590-8b27-4ff6-a6a0-02fe07d84f3b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) across Ads.

![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!h9Ik!,w_56,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F12043fbe-3bb2-4906-9595-9acea6400628_1280x1280.png)The Present Age

[Grok Can't Apologize. Grok Isn't Sentient. So Why Do Headlines Keep Saying It Did?](https://substack.com/redirect/70982b5c-1ca5-43a2-bb6e-b34f2453786c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)

Over the past week, users on X discovered something horrifying: strangers were replying to women’s photos and asking Grok, the platform’s built-in AI chatbot, to “remove her clothes” or “put her in a bikini.” And Grok was doing it. Publicly. In the replies. For everyone to see…

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!WMjD!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F605790ce-ce9b-44a1-a77e-bc12503caa70_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/1a454259-6613-4ddc-97ea-625c76d8a9d0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* The German Media Boss who [can’t find anything to buy](https://substack.com/redirect/f6edcff9-a675-4b8d-b50b-1d755e94ae8f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in the U.S.
* Channel 4 grows [streaming](https://substack.com/redirect/0b94c7ef-b6bf-4d43-bb05-04aba3bb4119?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) by 14%.
* A Rundown Of ([Pretty Much](https://substack.com/redirect/deee60b5-a15a-4ca2-a0c8-436bdc0f8ce6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)) Every Ad Tech Deal Of 2025.
* Nielsen, Roku [expand](https://substack.com/redirect/df426328-3ab5-462b-b8ab-19ff21bc69b1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) measurement partnership.
* ‘[Social listening](https://substack.com/redirect/c3f513ba-6e17-4f91-8842-cc06f1fa311f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’: Unilever seeks to capitalise on Vaseline’s TikTok moment.
* Adform acquires [Splicky](https://substack.com/redirect/781047e9-7360-4d49-920b-fa59390204a0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to strengthen its DACH presence.
* [LG](https://substack.com/redirect/e2194931-581e-4b61-b0d4-8f4e5f4bdb39?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is taking on Samsung’s ‘The Frame’ with its new ‘Gallery TV.’
* [Clicks](https://substack.com/redirect/f17bad27-0b28-44bd-8c77-86cf955e9c32?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is bringing its first smartphone and a new keyboard to CES 2026.

When you can also expense it:* [Generate](https://substack.com/redirect/7de831bd-d1e7-49f4-b5e5-a352df8893ab?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) an Employee Expense Email for Your Manager.

<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;margin-top: 0;">You know what, let’s start over. It’s for the best. 2026, baby!?</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>This newsletter is sponsored by our friends at </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/af0b9bfd-400f-400e-8363-fc60db92c74d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit</a><span>, the always-on, affordable brand tracking dashboard. </span><em>Now tracking 25 markets (and growing), giving you consistent brand health data everywhere you operate.</em></p>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Before you go further, Attention is like money–you can waste it or invest it. If you invest it in my paid newsletter, you'll get dividends for years.</p></blockquote>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/35a5bc13-d176-4382-8a9d-4ed2d925d23d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Meta’s Threads</a><span> makes a play for </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/190874a5-4ae5-41bb-aded-cd07aaa87d8d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">podcasters</a><span> and their rabid fans. </span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c45a28e3-306e-4dc5-9452-bfd20c62038b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Instagram CEO</a><span>: AI is so ubiquitous ‘it will be </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b6a302b9-4219-4ce1-9ffd-3596ef0cd152?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">more practical</a><span> to fingerprint real media than fake media’</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Snapchat launches new prompt-based “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/09d410a1-35a2-4d13-b8b1-49b139e3b94b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Animate It</a><span>” lens for paid subscribers. </span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bcb3070a-dca4-4e55-b45e-92fb682d21c2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Zuck</a><span> buys Chinese AI company </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bb689513-68cf-4862-92b2-918ca1153e33?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Manus</a><span> that claims it deals in actions, not words.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Elon Musk’s Grok AI floods X with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f8bd6781-d176-4eca-a17a-9cf49c5594e3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">sexualized photos</a><span> of women and minors.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>No, Grok can’t really “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/882bac48-cb72-4b55-b519-76c33fd7cd20?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">apologize</a><span>” for posting non-consensual sexual images</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Pinterest stock </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e0a0da19-0ac8-4a85-8119-ae933a5f1a9f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">rises</a><span> after </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/344c9efe-a80d-4ec8-881a-8ae3aa3667b3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">The Information</a><span> predicts OpenAI acquisition.</span></p></li>
</ul>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Twitter/X </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/00c84f5b-d278-487a-a8d8-8b8bbcc2f0fa?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">publishes</a><span> 2026 Marketing Calendar. </span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Italy regulator fines </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1506de27-05a2-40a2-8837-d99c0b171d76?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Apple $115 mln</a><span> for alleged anti-trust violations through app.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>How The Chernin Group’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/56c6e7f3-b373-4db0-874a-a3696bd108c8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">content-to-commerce bet</a><span> came crashing down.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>OpenAI’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/92f7b0bf-6a71-4fee-afe3-ec7009d06517?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">ChatGPT ads</a><span> will allegedly </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/82695d53-eee7-4621-a97c-dcc92e23e43a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">prioritize</a><span> sponsored content in answers.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Twitter and Pinterest founders </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7ad69d30-c4dd-490a-865f-c7d39041d168?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">launch app</a><span> as antidote to social media.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: rgb(0,0,0,.1);border: none;"/></div>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><em><span>In partnership with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9e758b3a-a8de-4498-b2a1-3cd37998247c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit</a></em></p></blockquote>
<h3 style="position: relative;font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: bold;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 1em 0 0.625em 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 1.16em;font-size: 1.375em;"><strong><span>The Bimma x Tracksuit: </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6ce90d4b-33fb-4029-aeb3-e8768ba7492a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: rgb(54,55,55);text-decoration: underline;">2025 Collab of the Year Report</a><span> is finally here…</span></strong></h3>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>For the first time, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2fc18039-4f89-4a43-a7b7-4b799559b306?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Bimma Williams</a><span> partnered with Tracksuit to measure sneaker collaborations the way consumers actually experience them, with real data and real cultural insight. They </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6ce90d4b-33fb-4029-aeb3-e8768ba7492a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">analyzed</a><span> the drops that moved people, shifted conversations, and built real brand energy.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>And built the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6ce90d4b-33fb-4029-aeb3-e8768ba7492a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">COLLAB Index</a><span>: an evaluation tool that assesses collaborations across six dimensions: Chemistry, Originality, Legacy, Leadership, Audience Engagement, and Brand Energy.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Most brands still pick collaborators based on vibes, but vibes alone don’t sell anything. The </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6ce90d4b-33fb-4029-aeb3-e8768ba7492a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">COLLAB Index</a><span> blends two data sources into one clear score that shows what worked </span><em>and</em><span> why:</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;">Consumer Reality: Tracksuit data from a survey of 2,000+ qualified US participants.</p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;">Cultural Reality: Evaluation from Bimma’s network of creators, journalists and strategists</p></li>
</ul>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><em><span>It’s the simplest way for marketers to understand, and prove, what makes a collaboration truly effective. If you’re planning a brand collab in 2026, don’t pitch it without this </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6ce90d4b-33fb-4029-aeb3-e8768ba7492a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Collab of the Year</a><span> report.</span></em></p></blockquote>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: rgb(0,0,0,.1);border: none;"/></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Salesforce executives say </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7bd1af86-27a7-48ab-85e4-8ce9bb7e20ca?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">trust in large language models</a><span> has declined.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/71c11497-fde9-4d6e-a6d2-99acd0ef9d5e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Disinformation</a><span> floods social media after Nicolás Maduro’s capture.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>FTC’s ad market </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ebb42330-cc48-4d3e-9f11-4ed4ac8fe4fe?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">crackdown</a><span>.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0a084945-4589-4cd0-8df6-6eb8018b69ff?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">US economy</a><span> grew at 4.3% rate in third quarter.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/93afca9f-ddc0-4fa4-ba40-900e987fe9aa?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Ozempic users</a><span> actually spend more dining out. Smart restaurants are adapting.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Bari Weiss’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/47ddbaa1-63ac-480b-9bdb-82434fe99eae?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Audience of One</a><span>: CBS News editor in chief defends held ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e5d20e1b-a301-44a3-900f-9cb5131e1f66?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">60 Minutes</a><span>’ story in email to CBS News staff.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>“Yo what?” LimeWire </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bd686736-768b-4855-80f8-8b95c4caa96a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">re-emerges</a><span> in online rush to share pulled “60 Minutes” segment.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Gender Politics and the Weaponization of Personal Data. (</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/cc3be126-ea53-48c8-bea9-845464e4658a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">read</a><span>)</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Someone made a huge profit </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/29b61a29-b031-4d3b-9aeb-4dbe2b895fd2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">predicting</a><span> Maduro’s capture.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>OpenAI is </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b84c197c-5532-46ad-9dd2-f8abcb7919f8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">paying employees</a><span> more than any major tech startup in history.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>In a year-end writeup on the state of AI, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e31bdf70-076f-443e-a98c-02bc1a08f26c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Microsoft CEO Satya Nadella</a><span> made it clear he’d really like us to stop saying “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/975c5e4c-7cb8-4026-be97-c543416b38a4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">slop</a><span>.”</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Dating apps are betting millions that</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e2e203a7-245a-4980-a334-24e85727edd0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> AI will convince you to fall back in love</a><span> with them.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3bcd7e06-1e8e-43cd-a38e-78936c9c77cb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">LinkedIn job scam</a><span> is global. The hook is local.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Volkswagen is bringing </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b4942f35-d441-4d12-bd58-4164776ca440?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">physical buttons back</a><span> to the dashboard with the ID. Polo EV.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>

<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bcf466fc-7ff9-48ba-b295-b62c07a73eb8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">FAO Schwarz</a><span> entices toy shoppers with the one thing they can’t get online.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>What media leaders say </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/44f95b1e-ed27-455f-9fb3-7301128b8a5c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">they got wrong</a><span> in 2025.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>OpenAI CEO Sam Altman just </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3a2cad18-2e49-4830-8378-b299ff68b403?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">publicly admitted</a><span> that AI agents are </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a45b9471-0ae0-4df8-aba2-d9c407d0b6ce?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">becoming</a><span> a problem.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c33eddb7-66b0-4cc3-98e3-24cca6413309?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">cost of AI slop</a><span> could cause a rethink that shakes the global economy in 2026.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>TikTok Shop launches </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/178b4b19-57fe-42fe-a9f7-371b3fa70dbe?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">digital gift cards</a><span> to challenge Amazon and eBay.</span></p></li>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/18433ee0-9fe7-4ca7-968c-3ab3252a132d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Poland</a><span> calls for EU action against </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9dea0222-dead-4811-97f4-60d531e2008a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI-generated TikTok videos</a><span> calling for “Polexit”.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>European Union </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8392f90d-1b34-43ae-aede-86d7297453f1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">responds</a><span> to TikTok videos calling for </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5aa73dd7-6c54-493e-9478-435730fd9c8c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Polexit</a><span>.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>TikTok returns as the official entertainment partner of the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/87a2fe9f-78c4-451d-9e17-70242768444a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">International Ski Federation (FIS)</a><span> after its premiere last year.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>TikTok’s Chinese owner plans </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5d1db8b0-0789-408d-b6d7-9730183f1416?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">$23bn AI spend</a><span> to keep pace with US rivals.</span></p></li>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>TikTok </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/482d074c-3780-454a-8920-fc7548db0ef0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">added</a><span> seasonal animations, personalized Year in Review summaries.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>TikTok Shop Malaysia to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/fc9b6d48-0ca8-4f61-b3d2-6f18c1216421?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">intensify</a><span> over</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/85d4cdb0-d51f-46c3-953e-7d6bb78f2fd1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> 1,000 All-Star Live Sessions</a><span> in 2026.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/91aefd84-4343-4623-98e1-1274a18f3700?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">A gamechanger</a><span>’: 200,000 UK small businesses sign up to TikTok Shop.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>China’s TikTok rival flooded with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/59fb4be3-20af-42c7-924e-c04339f84baf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">porn and violent video</a><span> in cyberattack.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>TikTok is jumping into </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c2754c79-8f7e-4fe4-9a44-b36f242629cc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">micro dramas</a><span>, the surging bite-sized soaps that have caught Hollywood’s eye.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Did Starmer </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/544b5481-2f05-4c2c-8306-a2d7297ea681?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">impose a curfew</a><span> in the U.K.? No, It’s a fake TikTok video.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>TikTok wants more creators to take a chance on brand campaigns. So it’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a3b5d7e8-140a-4360-99f5-5db1162ba9f2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">offering</a><span> $10,000.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>After watching X’s ownership issues play out, marketers brace for </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f8110798-d68d-470f-a005-99bedd06a037?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">TikTok whiplash</a><span> in 2026.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>ByteDance’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/30301b3c-e2b1-44a0-a794-19b1115a3dd8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">StoryMem</a><span> gives AI video models a memory so characters stop shapeshifting between scenes.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>ICYMI: Tiktok is scheduled to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8c1ea08c-1f60-4137-a5fc-d815bec3f7b6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">change ownership</a><span> on January 22, 2026.</span></p></li>
</ul>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/38bbecca-10e9-4755-880d-5cc0dc007b17?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Leaked documents</a><span> show Instagram’s plan to win back teens.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>What a </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e6bd5516-2c00-4f3a-9e38-6c16843ba001?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">14-year-old Instagram sensation</a><span> and six-hitting superstar tells us about life and cricket.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Instagram Stories is testing a new Close Friends feature for the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/52ac7d08-8305-4986-8db4-c1b445b6ac71?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Top 5 close friends</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>How </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b4d43dcb-ac9a-47df-a45c-d832c3777712?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Meta’s Reels</a><span> became a $50 billion business.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Instagram’s EU growth </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d97678fa-48b2-488b-9fb0-899b1dc3a1cd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">eclipses</a><span> Facebook.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Adam Mosseri calls out </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3c184513-2693-4b13-bfd7-b9df135b5844?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">camera companies</a><span> for going in the wrong direction.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0408689b-5080-4f5b-8bab-ad5b3c0d611e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">North West </a><span>is on Instagram.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Instagram on iOS is testing </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/af6f57f4-4641-4ee2-97e8-328f394db06b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">a new tab for profiles</a><span>, and it could be for playlists or possibly a reposts feed, similar to what TikTok tested a while ago.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>🦀 Report Break: Deloitte predicts that global ad revenue for “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4eccccfd-c26c-41f8-9c83-1f415ca892e5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">podcasts and vodcasts</a><span>” will reach $5bn in 2026, up 20% year-on-year. </span></p></blockquote>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3c0139af-6dee-4201-9cc1-7bd3fba86489?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Italy Watchdog</a><span> orders Meta to allow </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3d2fe809-4e97-49a7-a1be-a887b206ea0c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">rival AI Chatbots</a><span> in WhatsApp.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta says it will fully </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c6e96625-150f-45a6-80b8-59293dd21437?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">sever Manus’s China</a><span> ties after acquiring the AI startup.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5d69c0ff-cb34-42fd-b87e-08e394284d9c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Meta Ads Library</a><span> is now showing a label 'Low impression count' for ads that have generated less than 100 impressions.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>WhatsApp calls out </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/24fc8ecd-c350-4a27-a023-3dad4f94ee58?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">restrictions in Russia</a><span> after reported slowdown.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta’s Pixio proves </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5addafa1-e61b-438c-b946-ac69dfb146b4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">simple pixel reconstruction</a><span> can beat complex vision models.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>WhatsApp may soon allow </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ad4fb264-d0d0-4c72-9f69-0da3a4577e77?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">channel admins</a><span> to share invite links with contacts on iOS.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>This </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c1458d40-b5f7-4a6c-b18a-76aac84e2960?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">ad-free Facebook app</a><span> made my feed enjoyable again for the first time in years.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>8 WhatsApp features </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/fb70df89-940e-4ec5-bcb7-9c59e94de048?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">to</a><span> </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/fb70df89-940e-4ec5-bcb7-9c59e94de048?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">boost</a><span> your security and privacy.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Apple, Meta ship significantly </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/13c06e48-f1e6-4b1f-b70f-d9e752a40f2e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">fewer VR headsets</a><span> in 2025.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"></p>
</li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="727"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b64c6f06-642c-4817-816e-5d69564f13af?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/504f7135-86f7-4edd-a923-b08f71d3eeb3\_1100x220.png","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":220,"width":1100,"resizeWidth":727,"bytes":55200,"alt":null,"title":null,"type":"image/png","href":null,"belowTheFold":true,"topImage":false,"internalRedirect":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/i/163845782?img=https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F504f7135-86f7-4edd-a923-b08f71d3eeb3\_1100x220.png","isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="110" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!xdrk!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F504f7135-86f7-4edd-a923-b08f71d3eeb3\_1100x220.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>More than </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/531935c1-125d-43f2-b569-72aeca891422?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">20% of videos</a><span> shown to new YouTube users are ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/74b25f7b-52fe-4e02-890b-052860be0a4d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI slop</a><span>’, study finds.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Do </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/01f760e1-7ba2-4878-8a2e-ed6c63af25e8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">faces</a><span> help YouTube thumbnails? Here’s what the data says.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>In a recent post, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/aaf70dc2-6d62-4f6b-be4f-7cc44a28d140?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">vidIQ suggested</a><span> that unless you’re already well-known, people click for ideas rather than creators, and that removing your face from thumbnails can raise CTR.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/abd14265-6993-492b-904d-74a4684a165c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">relentless</a><span> rise of YouTube.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Seriously, Google? </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a8c35311-02f5-4158-9b8b-e4b7ce7fca09?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Uncensored porn</a><span> still appears on YouTube.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>This 22-year-old college dropout makes</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a0cf55ac-71b5-47e9-8cbe-330154147460?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> $700,000 a year</a><span> from ‘AI slop’ people sleep through.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>YouTube </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9fd98ce2-9019-4409-8513-29a460ff0399?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">launch</a><span> recap 2025 campaign​.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>YouTube TV is </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d9c1be27-36c9-41ca-9769-d490d96ad698?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">getting $20 cheaper</a><span> for select users thanks to a new partnership.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>🫒 Report Break: The Italian TV market is projected to reach a value of </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/99c3e93a-72ec-4287-9395-b071ca85401f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">€8.97 billion by the end of 2025</a><span>, representing a 2.2% growth over 2024.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">While linear TV advertising is projected to experience a decline of 0.4% in 2025, non-linear online advertising is set to register nearly 30% growth.</p>
</blockquote>
